# TownKit hook list

_Generated by `townkit/hookgen` from `towntopia-0.1.3.jar`. Do not edit by hand._

7953 hooks in 1240 classes. Use the key with `TownKitHooks.before(key, ...)` / `after(key, ...)`.

Skipped:

- class: Towntopia test kit, datagen and mixins: 36
- class: anonymous (unstable names): 142
- class: synthetic: 117
- method: abstract/native: 125
- method: data-class constructor: 404
- method: enum boilerplate: 518
- method: equals/hashCode/toString: 1969
- method: hot path (rules file): 324
- method: packet plumbing: 2
- method: plain getter/setter: 3339
- method: private method: 7370
- method: static initializer: 839
- method: synthetic (lambdas, bridges): 2588

Carrier-only classes (no hooks, but TownKit's check-site rewrite needs them; 1 from `@carrier` rules, 1 added because building-sites.tsv, nav-sites.tsv or work-sites.tsv name them as rewritten owners and the trim left them without hooks): `village.NavCells$1`, `village.villagers.blacksmith.behavior.BlacksmithActivity$Mat`

Code size as callers see it (measured by running the real injector on the jar). A hooked method keeps its body byte for byte under a private name and callers reach it through a small wrapper; constructors are hooked in place.

- 7953 hooked methods: size change seen by callers -504442 bytes in total; median -13, 90th percentile +14, max +53 bytes
- crossed 35 bytes (no longer inlined into lukewarm callers): 118, 117 of them constructors
- crossed 325 bytes (no longer inlined into hot callers): 0
- over 8000 bytes (never JIT-compiled, already so before hooking): 0
- hooks dropped to keep a method compilable: 0

Classes counted as client-only because their own signatures name Minecraft client types (check these after each Towntopia update: a shared class here would lose its server hooks): DynamicVillagesClient

Rules in hook-rules.txt that matched nothing: none
